# META
~~~ini
description=Usage evaluation follows effective part redefinitions while leaving the architecture's authored roll-up independent of concrete integrations
type=file
libraries=standard
require_complete_publication=true
require_no_diagnostics=true
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package Rollup {
    private import ISQ::*;
    private import SI::*;
    part def FrameSpec { attribute mass : MassValue; }
    part def LightFrame :> FrameSpec { attribute :>> mass = 7.5 [kg]; }
    part def HeavyFrame :> FrameSpec { attribute :>> mass = 9.8 [kg]; }
    part def SupplySpec { attribute mass : MassValue; }
    part def SmallSupply :> SupplySpec { attribute :>> mass = 0.9 [kg]; }

    part def ProductArchitecture {
        part chassis : FrameSpec;
        part supply : SupplySpec;
        attribute totalMass : MassValue = chassis.mass + supply.mass;
    }
    part def LightIntegration :> ProductArchitecture {
        part :>> chassis : LightFrame;
        part :>> supply : SmallSupply;
    }
    part def HeavyIntegration :> ProductArchitecture {
        part :>> chassis : HeavyFrame;
        part :>> supply : SmallSupply;
    }
    part light : LightIntegration;
    part heavy : HeavyIntegration;
    part incomplete : ProductArchitecture;
}
~~~
